{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787313345758,"sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"skill","callID":"call-a0101427-9fbb-4c58-801f-c58fdda702f1-0","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"name":"fastapi-python"},"output":"<skill_content name=\"fastapi-python\">\n# Skill: fastapi-python\n\n# FastAPI Python\n\nYou are an expert in FastAPI and Python backend development.\n\n## Key Principles\n\n- Write concise, technical responses with accurate Python examples\n- Favor functional, declarative programming over class-based approaches\n- Prioritize modularization to eliminate code duplication\n- Use descriptive variable names with auxiliary verbs (e.g., `is_active`, `has_permission`)\n- Employ lowercase with underscores for file/directory naming (e.g., `routers/user_routes.py`)\n- Export routes and utilities explicitly\n- Follow the RORO (Receive an Object, Return an Object) pattern\n\n## Python/FastAPI Standards\n\n- Use `def` for pure functions, `async def` for asynchronous operations\n- Use type hints for all function signatures. Prefer Pydantic models over raw dictionaries\n- Structure: exported router, sub-routes, utilities, static content, types (models, schemas)\n- Omit curly braces for single-line conditionals\n- Write concise one-line conditional syntax\n\n## Error Handling\n\n- Handle edge cases at function entry points\n- Employ early returns for error conditions\n- Place happy path logic last\n- Avoid unnecessary else statements; use if-return patterns\n- Implement guard clauses for preconditions\n- Provide proper error logging and user-friendly messaging\n\n## FastAPI-Specific Guidelines\n\n- Use functional components (plain functions) and Pydantic models for input validation\n- Declare routes with clear return type annotations\n- Prefer lifespan context managers for managing startup and shutdown events\n- Leverage middleware for logging, error monitoring, and optimization\n- Use HTTPException for expected errors and model them as specific HTTP responses\n- Apply Pydantic's BaseModel consistently for validation\n\n## Performance Optimization\n\n- Minimize blocking I/O; use async for all database and API calls\n- Implement caching with Redis or in-memory stores\n- Optimize Pydantic serialization/deserialization\n- Use lazy loading for large datasets\n\n## Key Conventions\n\n1. Rely on FastAPI's dependency injection system\n2. Prioritize API performance metrics (response time, latency, throughput)\n3. Structure routes and dependencies for readability and maintainability\n\n## Dependencies\n\nFastAPI, Pydantic v2, asyncpg/aiomysql, SQLAlchemy 2.0\n\nBase directory for this skill: /workspaces/powerchoice/.agents/skills/fastapi-python\nRelative paths in this skill (e.g., scripts/, reference/) are relative to this base directory.\nNote: file list is sampled.\n\n<skill_files>\n\n</skill_files>\n</skill_content>","metadata":{"name":"fastapi-python","dir":"/workspaces/powerchoice/.agents/skills/fastapi-python","truncated":false},"title":"Loaded skill: fastapi-python","time":{"start":1787313344506,"end":1787313344719}},"id":"prt_0242d4faf001MQstuX4dzGs702","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242d294f00136pZxA5T1H29OE"}}

export type RankingTargetMode = 'winner' | 'top_n' | 'full'\n6: \n7: export type PairAnswer = {\n8:   itemA: number\n9:   itemB: number\n10:   winnerId: number | null\n11:   response: hashSeed(parts: Array<string | number>): number {\n34:   const s = parts.join('|')\n35:   let h = 2166136261\n36:   for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {\n37:     h ^= s.charCodeAt(i)\n38:     h = Math.imul(h, 16777619)\n39:   }\n40:   return h >>> 0\n41: }\n42: \n43: function seededUnit(seed: number): number {\n44:   const x = Math.sin(seed) * 10000\n45:   return x - Math.floor(x)\n46: }\n47: \n48: export function fitProvisionalScores(items: number[], answers: PairAnswer[]): Map<number, number> {\n49:   const scores = new Map<number, number>()\n50:   for (const id of items) scores.set(id, 0)\n51:   const usable = answers.filter(a => a.response === 'winner' || a.response === 'tie')\n52:   if (!usable.length) return scores\n53:   const lr = 0.25\n54:   const reg = 0.08\n55:   for (let iter = 0; iter < 24; iter++) {\n56:     const grad = new Map<number, number>()\n57:     for (const id of items) grad.set(id, reg * (scores.get(id) || 0))\n58:     for (const ans of usable) {\n59:       const sa = scores.get(ans.itemA) || 0\n60:       const sb = scores.get(ans.itemB) || 0\n61:       const p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-(sa - sb)))\n62:       const y = ans.response === 'tie' ? 0.5 : (ans.winnerId === ans.itemA ? 1 : 0)\n63:       const g = p - y\n64:       grad.set(ans.itemA, (grad.get(ans.itemA) || 0) + g)\n65:       grad.set(ans.itemB, (grad.get(ans.itemB) || 0) - g)\n66:     }\n67:     for (const id of items) {\n68:       scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) || 0) - lr * (grad.get(id) || 0))\n69:     }\n70:     const mean = items.reduce((s, id) => s + (scores.get(id) || 0), 0) / items.length\n71:     for (const id of items) scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) || 0) - mean)\n72:   }\n73:   return scores\n74: }\n75: \n76: function ranksFromScores(items: number[], scores: Map<number, number>): Map<number, number> {\n77:   const ordered = [...items].sort((a, b) => {\n78:     const d = (scores.get(b) || 0) - (scores.get(a) || 0)\n79:     return d !== 0 ? d : a - b\n80:   })\n81:   const ranks = new Map<number, number>()\n82:   ordered.forEach((id, i) => ranks.set(id, i + 1))\n83:   return ranks\n84: }\n85: \n86: function components(items: number[], edges: Array<[number, number]>): number[][] {\n87:   const adj = new Map<number, number[]>()\n88:   for (const id of items) adj.set(id, [])\n89:   for (const [a, b] of edges) {\n90:     adj.get(a)?.push(b)\n91:     adj.get(b)?.push(a)\n92:   }\n93:   const seen = new Set<number>()\n94:   const out: number[][] = []\n95:   for (const id of items) {\n96:     if (seen.has(id)) continue\n97:     const stack = [id]\n98:     const comp: number[] = []\n99:     seen.add(id)\n100:     while (stack.length) {\n101:       const cur = stack.pop()!\n102:       comp.push(cur)\n103:       for (const nxt of adj.get(cur) || []) {\n104:         if (!seen.has(nxt)) {\n105:           seen.add(nxt)\n106:           stack.push(nxt)\n107:         }\n108:       }\n109:     }\n110:     out.push(comp)\n111:   }\n112:   return out\n113: }\n114: \n115: function usedPairSet(answers: PairAnswer[]): Set<string> {\n116:   const s = new Set<string>()\n117:   for (const a of answers) s.add(pairKey(a.itemA, a.itemB))\n118:   return s\n119: }\n120: \n121: function historicalStats(prior: PairAnswer[], a: number, b: number) {\n122:   let count = 0\n123:   let winsA = 0\n124:   let winsB = 0\n125:   for (const p of prior) {\n126:     if (pairKey(p.itemA, p.itemB) !== pairKey(a, b)) continue\n127:     count++\n128:     if (p.response !== 'winner' || p.winnerId == null) continue\n129:     if (p.winnerId === a) winsA++\n130:     else if (p.winnerId === b) winsB++\n131:   }\n132:   return { count, winsA, winsB }\n133: }\n134: \n135: export function enumerateLegalPairs(state: SchedulerState): Array<[number, number]> {\n136:   const used = usedPairSet(state.answers)\n137:   const last = state.lastPair\n138:   const all: Array<[number, number]> = []\n139:   const disjoint: Array<[number, number]> = []\n140:   for (let i = 0; i < state.items.length; i++) {\n141:     for (let j = i + 1; j < state.items.length; j++) {\n142:       const a = state.items[i]\n143:       const b = state.items[j]\n144:       if (used.has(pairKey(a, b))) continue\n145:       all.push([a, b])\n146:       if (!last || (a !== last[0] && a !== last[1] && b !== last[0] && b !== last[1])) {\n147:         disjoint.push([a, b])\n148:       }\n149:     }\n150:   }\n\n(Showing lines 1-150 of 309. 'winner' | 'top_n' | 'full'\n\nexport type PairAnswer = {\n  itemA: number\n  itemB: number\n  winnerId: number | null\n  response: PairResponse\n  presentedLeftId: number\n  presentedRightId: number\n  decisionSeconds: number\n}\n\nexport type RankingTarget = { mode: RankingTargetMode; n?: number }\n\nexport type SchedulerState = {\n  items: number[]\n  target: RankingTarget\n  passIndex: number\n  questionBudget: number\n  answers: PairAnswer[]\n  priorAnswers: PairAnswer[]\n  lastPair: [number, number] | null\n}\n\nfunction pairKey(a: number, b: number): string {\n  return a < b ? `${a},${b}` : `${b},${a}`\n}\n\nfunction hashSeed(parts: Array<string | number>): number {\n  const s = parts.join('|')\n  let h = 2166136261\n  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {\n    h ^= s.charCodeAt(i)\n    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619)\n  }\n  return h >>> 0\n}\n\nfunction seededUnit(seed: number): number {\n  const x = Math.sin(seed) * 10000\n  return x - Math.floor(x)\n}\n\nexport function fitProvisionalScores(items: number[], answers: PairAnswer[]): Map<number, number> {\n  const scores = new Map<number, number>()\n  for (const id of items) scores.set(id, 0)\n  const usable = answers.filter(a => a.response === 'winner' || a.response === 'tie')\n  if (!usable.length) return scores\n  const lr = 0.25\n  const reg = 0.08\n  for (let iter = 0; iter < 24; iter++) {\n    const grad = new Map<number, number>()\n    for (const id of items) grad.set(id, reg * (scores.get(id) || 0))\n    for (const ans of usable) {\n      const sa = scores.get(ans.itemA) || 0\n      const sb = scores.get(ans.itemB) || 0\n      const p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-(sa - sb)))\n      const y = ans.response === 'tie' ? 0.5 : (ans.winnerId === ans.itemA ? 1 : 0)\n      const g = p - y\n      grad.set(ans.itemA, (grad.get(ans.itemA) || 0) + g)\n      grad.set(ans.itemB, (grad.get(ans.itemB) || 0) - g)\n    }\n    for (const id of items) {\n      scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) || 0) - lr * (grad.get(id) || 0))\n    }\n    const mean = items.reduce((s, id) => s + (scores.get(id) || 0), 0) / items.length\n    for (const id of items) scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) || 0) - mean)\n  }\n  return scores\n}\n\nfunction ranksFromScores(items: number[], scores: Map<number, number>): Map<number, number> {\n  const ordered = [...items].sort((a, b) => {\n    const d = (scores.get(b) || 0) - (scores.get(a) || 0)\n    return d !== 0 ? d : a - b\n  })\n  const ranks = new Map<number, number>()\n  ordered.forEach((id, i) => ranks.set(id, i + 1))\n  return ranks\n}\n\nfunction components(items: number[], edges: Array<[number, number]>): number[][] {\n  const adj = new Map<number, number[]>()\n  for (const id of items) adj.set(id, [])\n  for (const [a, b] of edges) {\n    adj.get(a)?.push(b)\n    adj.get(b)?.push(a)\n  }\n  const seen = new Set<number>()\n  const out: number[][] = []\n  for (const id of items) {\n    if (seen.has(id)) continue\n    const stack = [id]\n    const comp: number[] = []\n    seen.add(id)\n    while (stack.length) {\n      const cur = stack.pop()!\n      comp.push(cur)\n      for (const nxt of adj.get(cur) || []) {\n        if (!seen.has(nxt)) {\n          seen.add(nxt)\n          stack.push(nxt)\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    out.push(comp)\n  }\n  return out\n}\n\nfunction usedPairSet(answers: PairAnswer[]): Set<string> {\n  const s = new Set<string>()\n  for (const a of answers) s.add(pairKey(a.itemA, a.itemB))\n  return s\n}\n\nfunction historicalStats(prior: PairAnswer[], a: number, b: number) {\n  let count = 0\n  let winsA = 0\n  let winsB = 0\n  for (const p of prior) {\n    if (pairKey(p.itemA, p.itemB) !== pairKey(a, b)) continue\n    count++\n    if (p.response !== 'winner' || p.winnerId == null) continue\n    if (p.winnerId === a) winsA++\n    else if (p.winnerId === b) winsB++\n  }\n  return { count, winsA, winsB }\n}\n\nexport function enumerateLegalPairs(state: SchedulerState): Array<[number, number]> {\n  const used = usedPairSet(state.answers)\n  const last = state.lastPair\n  const all: Array<[number, number]> = []\n  const disjoint: Array<[number, number]> = []\n  for (let i = 0; i < state.items.length; i++) {\n    for (let j = i + 1; j < state.items.length; j++) {\n      const a = state.items[i]\n      const b = state.items[j]\n      if (used.has(pairKey(a, b))) continue\n      all.push([a, b])\n      if (!last || (a !== last[0] && a !== last[1] && b !== last[0] && b !== last[1])) {\n        disjoint.push([a, b])\n      }\n    }\n  }","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":150,"totalLines":309,"truncated":true}},"title":"client/utils/pairScheduler.ts","time":{"start":1787313352949,"end":1787313354466}},"id":"prt_0242d6f2b001P4dKUP1CmCtej4","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242d5f83001dQCo4YStzxB0JQ"}}

function bootstrap() {\n  loading.value = true\n  try {\n    await loadProject(projectId.value, { myVote: false })\n    const res = await votes.getMyVote(projectId.value, true) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to load compare session'\n  } finally {\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function submitCompletedGroup() {\n  const pkg = runner.buildPackage()\n  if (!pkg) return\n  fetching.value = true\n  submitting.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votes.completeGroup(projectId.value, pkg) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to save group'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n    submitting.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nwatch(\n  () => runner.complete.value,\n  async (done) => {\n    if (done && runner.group.value) await submitCompletedGroup()\n  },\n)\n\nconst lastFlushedCount = ref(0)\nlet idleTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null\n\nfunction flushThresholds() {\n  return {\n    count: Math.max(1, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_flush_count || 5)),\n    idleMs: Math.max(5, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_idle_seconds || 90)) * 1000,\n  }\n}\n\nasync function flushPartial() {\n  if (runner.complete.value || submitting.value) return\n  const pkg = runner.buildPartialPackage()\n  if (!pkg || !pkg.pairings.length) return\n  if (pkg.pairings.length === lastFlushedCount.value) return\n  try {\n    await votes.saveGroup(projectId.value, pkg)\n    lastFlushedCount.value = pkg.pairings.length\n  } catch { /* best-effort */ }\n}\n\nwatch(\n  () => runner.inGroupDone.value,\n  (n) => {\n    if (idleTimer) clearTimeout(idleTimer)\n    if (!n || runner.complete.value) return\n    const { count, idleMs } = flushThresholds()\n    if (n - lastFlushedCount.value >= count) void flushPartial()\n    idleTimer = setTimeout(() => { void flushPartial() }, idleMs)\n  },\n)\n\nonBeforeUnmount(() => {\n  if (idleTimer) clearTimeout(idleTimer)\n})\n\nfunction onUndo() {\n  runner.undo()\n}\n\nfunction onPause() {\n  paused.value = true\n  runner.setPaused(true)\n}\n\nfunction onResume() {\n  paused.value = false\n  runner.setPaused(false)\n}\n\nasync function markComplete() {\n  submitting.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votes.markComplete(projectId.value, true) as any\n    votesCaptured.value = Number(res.votes_captured || 0)\n    completeView.value = true\n    maxCompleteView.value = false\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    heldNextGroup.value = null\n    runner.stop()\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to mark complete'\n  } finally {\n    submitting.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function continuePastMax() {\n  maxCompleteAcknowledged.value = true\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  const held = heldNextGroup.value\n  heldNextGroup.value = null\n  if (held) {\n    beginGroup(held)\n    return\n  }\n  fetching.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votes.nextGroup(projectId.value) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to continue comparisons'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function resumeAfterComplete() {\n  fetching.value = true\n  try {\n    await votes.markComplete(projectId.value, false)\n    completeView.value = false\n    maxCompleteAcknowledged.value = true\n    const res = await votes.nextGroup(projectId.value) as any\n    await applySession(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to resume comparisons'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {\n  if (completeView.value || paused.value || maxCompleteView.value || showGroupIntro.value || !runner.left.value) return\n  if (e.key === '1') runner.answer('winner', runner.left.value.id)\n  else if (e.key === '2') runner.answer('winner', runner.right.value!.id)\n  else if (e.key === 'e' || e.key === 'E') runner.answer('tie')\n  else if (e.key === 'n' || e.key === 'N') runner.answer('unsure')\n  else if (e.key === 'u' || e.key === 'U') onUndo()\n}\n\nonMounted(() => {\n  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)\n  bootstrap()\n})\nonBeforeUnmount(() => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey))\n</script>","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":473,"totalLines":473,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/pages/projects/[id]/probe.vue","time":{"start":1787313358966,"end":1787313359325}},"id":"prt_0242d87aa0017yqwj2ZJdwhrBL","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242d7cdc001Va173LjWPLi44x"}}

Accepts 0–1 or 0–100. */\nexport function metricLabel(value: number) {\n  const v = asMetricPercent(value)\n  if (v >= 80) return 'Strong'\n  if (v >= 60) return 'Solid'\n  if (v >= 40) return 'Building'\n  if (v >= 20) return 'Early'\n  return 'Sparse'\n}\n\n/**\n * Format a ranking score as percent.\n * Server scores are normalized preference in [0, 1].\n * Values already > 1 are treated as percent points (defensive).\n */\nexport function formatPercent(score: number) {\n  const n = Number(score)\n  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '0%'\n  if (n <= 1) return `${Math.round(n * 100)}%`\n  return `${Math.round(n)}%`\n}\n\n/** Bar width 0–100 from a 0–1 (or 0–100) score. */\nexport function scoreBarPercent(score: number) {\n  const n = Number(score)\n  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0\n  if (n <= 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n * 100))\n  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n))\n}\n\n/** Lead between two 0–1 scores as integer \"points\" (percent points). */\nexport function scoreLeadPoints(a: number, b: number) {\n  return Math.round(Math.abs(scoreBarPercent(a) - scoreBarPercent(b)))\n}\n\nexport function effectiveCriteria(criteria: RankItem[]): RankItem[] {\n  const active = (criteria || []).filter(c => c.active !== false)\n  if (active.length) return active\n  return [{ id: 0, title: 'Overall' }]\n}","lineStart":90,"lineEnd":139,"totalLines":529,"truncated":true}},"title":"client/utils/ranking.ts","time":{"start":1787313359821,"end":1787313360049}},"id":"prt_0242d8bae001DY6yg4FcsN5L4g","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242d7cdc001Va173LjWPLi44x"}}

not None else sum(\n        int(g.get(\"comparison_count\") or len(g.get(\"pairings\") or []) or 0) for g in done_groups\n    )\n    completed_pass = max_completed_pass(done_groups)\n    current_pass = completed_pass\n    all_opts = list(option_ids)\n    active_opts = list(all_opts)\n    factor_n = len(factor_ids)\n    opt_n = max(0, len(active_opts))\n    opt_budget, fac_budget = resolved_question_budgets(project, opt_n, factor_n)\n\n    def _remaining_slots(pass_index: int) -> list[dict]:\n        slots = required_slots_for_pass(\n            pass_index=pass_index,\n            alternative_ids=active_opts,\n            factor_ids=factor_ids,\n            option_question_budget=opt_budget,\n            factor_question_budget=fac_budget,\n            rng=random.Random(pass_index),\n        )\n        return [\n            s for s in slots\n            if not (set(s[\"item_ids\"]) <= channel_covered_item_ids(\n                done_groups,\n                pass_index=pass_index,\n                group_type=s[\"group_type\"],\n                criterion_id=s[\"criterion_id\"],\n            ))\n        ]\n\n    if completed_pass == 0:\n        current_pass = 1\n    else:\n        remaining = _remaining_slots(completed_pass)\n        current_pass = completed_pass if remaining else completed_pass + 1\n\n    def comps_for_future_pass(p: int) -> int:\n        return estimate_pass_comparisons(opt_n, factor_n, opt_budget, fac_budget)\n\n    # remaining in current pass\n    remaining_slots = _remaining_slots(current_pass)\n    remaining_comps = 0\n    for s in remaining_slots:\n        remaining_comps += int(s.get(\"question_budget\") or estimated_comparisons(len(s[\"item_ids\"])))\n\n    target_max = ps[\"max_recommended_passes\"]\n    target_min = ps[\"min_expected_passes\"]\n    hard = ps[\"hard_max_passes\"]\n\n    def comps_done_through_pass(end_pass: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Actual comparisons recorded in completed groups up to end_pass inclusive.\"\"\"\n        total = 0\n        for g in done_groups:\n            if int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) <= end_pass:\n                total += int(g.get(\"comparison_count\") or len(g.get(\"pairings\") or []) or 0)\n        return total\n\n    def total_to_pass(end_pass: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Estimate total comparisons from session start through end of end_pass.\"\"\"\n        if end_pass < 1:\n            return 0\n        # Already past this target: freeze at work actually done through that pass\n        # (do not inflate with optional later-pass remaining work).\n        if current_pass > end_pass:\n            return comps_done_through_pass(end_pass)\n        # Still working toward end_pass: done so far + rest of current + future full passes\n        t = c_done + remaining_comps\n        for p in range(current_pass + 1, end_pass + 1):\n            t += comps_for_future_pass(p)\n        return t\n\n    est_at_min = total_to_pass(target_min)\n    est_at_max = total_to_pass(target_max)\n    est_total = est_at_max  # primary overall bar targets recommended max\n\n    # When still short of a target, keep estimate >= done so the bar never exceeds 100%\n    # early if the participant overshot the algorithm upper bound. Once past the target\n    # pass, leave the frozen total as-is so the UI can stay full on optional work.\n    if current_pass <= target_min:\n        est_at_min = max(est_at_min, c_done)\n    if current_pass <= target_max:\n        est_at_max = max(est_at_max, c_done)\n        est_total = max(est_total, c_done)\n\n    return {\n        \"participant_comparisons_done\": c_done,\n null,\n      sort_algorithm: String(g.sort_algorithm || 'ford_johnson'),\n      item_ids_initial: ids,\n      rank_order: includeRank ? rankOrderFromAnswers(ids, answers.value) : [],\n      pairings: answers.value.map(toPairingPayload),\n      algorithm_version: g.algorithm_version || '1.1-adaptive',\n      event_timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n    }\n  }\n\n  function buildPackage() {\n    if (!complete.value) return null\n    const pkg = buildPackageBody(true)\n    const g = group.value\n    if (g) clearLocal(tokenOf(g))\n    return pkg\n  }\n\n  function buildPartialPackage() {\n    if (!answers.value.length) return null\n    return buildPackageBody(false)\n  }\n\n  function stop() {\n    group.value = null\n    left.value = null\n    right.value = null\n    answers.value = []\n    priorAnswers.value = []\n  }\n\n  return {\n    group,\n    left,\n    right,\n    inGroupDone,\n    inGroupTotal,\n    complete,\n    paused,\n    pairings,\n    startGroup,\n    answer,\n    undo,\n    setPaused,\n    buildPackage,\n    buildPartialPackage,\n    stop,\n    canUndo: computed(() => answers.value.length > 0 && !complete.value),\n  }\n}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":322,"totalLines":322,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/composables/useSortGroupRunner.ts","time":{"start":1787313359767,"end":1787313360853}},"id":"prt_0242d8a76001UkDsw4KrpJar5G","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242d7cdc001Va173LjWPLi44x"}}

d.get(\"description\")\n\n\ndef serialize_item(d: dict) -> dict:\n    return {\n        \"id\": _item_id(d),\n        \"title\": _item_title(d),\n        \"description\": _item_description(d),\n    }\n\n\ndef serialize_criterion(d: dict) -> dict:\n    return {\n        \"id\": _item_id(d),\n        \"title\": _item_title(d),\n        \"description\": _item_description(d),\n        \"comparison_question\": normalize_comparison_question(d.get(\"comparison_question\")),\n    }\n\n\ndef new_group_token(length: int = 10) -> str:\n    return \"\".join(secrets.choice(GROUP_TOKEN_ALPHABET) for _ in range(length))\n\n\ndef normalize_ranking_mode(mode: Any, *, exclusive_mode: bool | None = None) -> str:\n    raw = mode.value if hasattr(mode, \"value\") else mode\n    m = str(raw or \"\").strip().lower().replace(\"-\", \"_\").replace(\" \", \"_\")\n    aliases = {\n        \"winner\": \"find_best\",\n        \"pick_one\": \"find_best\",\n        \"exclusive\": \"find_best\",\n        \"top_3\": \"find_top_3\",\n        \"top3\": \"find_top_3\",\n        \"findtop3\": \"find_top_3\",\n        \"top_half\": \"find_top_half\",\n RANKING_MODES:\n        return m\n    if exclusive_mode:\n        return \"find_best\"\n    return \"rank_all\"\n\n\ndef exclusive_from_ranking_mode(mode: Any) -> bool:\n    return normalize_ranking_mode(mode) == \"find_best\"\n\n\ndef resolve_ranking_target(\n    project: Any,\n    *,\n    n_items: int,\n    group_type: str,\n) -> tuple[str, int | None]:\n    if str(group_type) == \"criteria\":\n        return \"full\", None\n    mode = normalize_ranking_mode(\n        getattr(project, \"ranking_mode\", None),\n        exclusive_mode=bool(getattr(project, \"project_exclusive_mode\", False)),\n    )\n    n = max(0, int(n_items or 0))\n    if mode == \"find_best\":\n        return \"winner\", 1\n    if mode == \"find_top_3\":\n        return \"top_n\", min(3, max(1, n // 2)) if n else 1\n    if mode == \"find_top_half\":\n        return \"top_n\", max(1, n // 2) if n else 1\n    return \"full\", None\n\n\ndef project_ranking_mode(project: Any) -> str:\n    return normalize_ranking_mode(\n        getattr(project, \"ranking_mode\", None),\n        exclusive_mode=bool(getattr(project, \"project_exclusive_mode\", False)),\n    )\n\n\ndef project_pass_settings(project: Any) -> dict:\n    mn = clamp_min_expected_passes(getattr(project, \"min_expected_passes\", None))\n    mx = clamp_max_recommended_passes(getattr(project, \"max_recommended_passes\", None), mn)\n    return {\n        \"min_expected_passes\": mn,\n        \"max_recommended_passes\": mx,\n        \"hard_max_passes\": hard_max_passes(mx, mn),\n        \"option_questions_per_group\": clamp_questions_per_group_stored(\n            getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group\", None)\n        ),\n        \"factor_questions_per_group\": \"option_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)\n        ),\n        \"factor_questions_per_group_explicit\": bool(\n            getattr(project, \"factor_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)\n        ),\n        \"ranking_mode\": project_ranking_mode(project),\n    }\n\n\ndef resolved_question_budgets(project: Any, option_n: int, factor_n: int) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    return (\n        resolve_questions_per_group(\n            getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group\", None),\n            option_n,\n            bool(getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group_explicit\", \"factor_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)),\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef _is_complete_group(g: dict) -> bool:\n    status = g.get(\"status\")\n    if hasattr(status, \"value\"):\n        status = {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787313370922,"sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-168b5dd5-33f0-4bf9-b686-ccb408ae5e2c-27","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md","limit":80},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # Power Choice Pro — terminology dictionary\n2: \n3: Canonical **user-facing** language for the product. Use this for UI copy, empty states, emails, and OpenAPI/field *descriptions*.\n4: \n5: **Scope**\n6: \n7: - Apply consistently in the SPA and human-readable API docs.\n8: - Do **not** rename database columns, JSON field names, path segments, or enums unless a change is local and very low risk.\n9: - Internal code may keep `alternative`, `criteria`, `observation`, `probe`, `vote` as implementation vocabulary.\n10: \n11: **Related product nouns (out of this glossary)**\n12: \n13: - **Application** / API keys — toolkit integrations, not decision projects.\n14: - **Workspace** — company/account context (signup), not a project.\n15: \n16: ---\n17: \n18: ## Principles\n19: \n20: 1. One primary noun per concept in user-facing copy.\n21: 2. Everyday English first; avoid MCDA jargon in the main UI.\n22: 3. Verbs for actions, nouns for things: *compare options using factors*.\n23: 4. Brand-safe: “choice” is product flavor (Power Choice), not the name of an option or mode.\n24: 5. Top-level work unit: **Project**. Primary action: **Compare** / **comparison**.\n25: \n26: ---\n27: \n28: ## Canonical terms\n29: \n30: ### 1. Container & framing\n31: \n32: | Role | Canonical | Notes |\n33: |------|-----------|--------|\n34: | Owned work unit | **Project** | Nav, lists, CRUD, routes |\n35: | What it’s about | **decision** | Framing only (“Structure the decision”) — not a second entity |\n36: | Account context | **Workspace** | Company/signup only |\n37: \n38: ### 2. Project mode\n39: \n40: | Role | Canonical | Internal |\n41: |------|-----------|----------|\n42: | Single winner | **Pick one** | `project_exclusive_mode = true` |\n43: | Full ordering | **Rank all** | `project_exclusive_mode = false` |\n44: | Edit labels | **Choose one winner** / **Rank all options** | OK long form on edit |\n45: \n46: Avoid in UI: Choice, Prioritization, exclusive, selection-mode.\n47: \n48: ### 3. Things being compared\n49: \n50: | Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n51: |------|-----------|-----------------|\n52: | Entity | **Option** | `alternative_*`, `CustomerProjectAlternatives` |\n53: | Count / empty states | **options** | — |\n54: \n55: Avoid in UI: alternative, choice (as the item), item.\n56: \n57: ### 4. Evaluation dimensions\n58: \n59: | Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n60: |------|-----------|-----------------|\n61: | Entity | **Factor** | `factor_*` |\n62: | Section | **Factors** | — |\n63: | No separate factors | **Overall only** | Synthetic factor title: **Overall** |\n64: | Multi | **Multiple factors** | — |\n65: | Templates | **Template** | Project / options / factors scopes; internal: `CustomerFactorTemplates`, `project_templates.yml` |\n66: \n67: Avoid in UI: criteria, criterion, rubric (except rare help metaphor).\n68: \n69: Observation type `criteria` means factor-vs-factor comparisons — never say “criteria” to users.\n70: \n71: ### 5. Comparing (giving input)\n72: \n73: | Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n74: |------|-----------|-----------------|\n75: | Verb / CTA | **Compare** / **Start comparing** | vote APIs, probe routes |\n76: | One unit | **comparison** | `observation`, probe |\n77: | Progress | **comparisons** | — |\n78: | Finish | **Mark complete** | — |\n79: | Locked | **Input closed** / **Comparisons locked** | `disabled` |\n80: | Status collecting | **Collecting input** | — |\n\n(Showing lines 1-80 of 226. Use offset=81 to continue.)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# Power Choice Pro — terminology dictionary\n\nCanonical **user-facing** language for the product. Use this for UI copy, empty states, emails, and OpenAPI/field *descriptions*.\n\n**Scope**\n\n- Apply consistently in the SPA and human-readable API docs.\n- Do **not** rename database columns, JSON field names, path segments, or enums unless a change is local and very low risk.\n- Internal code may keep `alternative`, `criteria`, `observation`, `probe`, `vote` as implementation vocabulary.\n\n**Related product nouns (out of this glossary)**\n\n- **Application** / API keys — toolkit integrations, not decision projects.\n- **Workspace** — company/account context (signup), not a project.\n\n---\n\n## Principles\n\n1. One primary noun per concept in user-facing copy.","truncated":true,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md","text":"# Power Choice Pro — terminology dictionary\n\nCanonical **user-facing** language for the product. Use this for UI copy, empty states, emails, and OpenAPI/field *descriptions*.\n\n**Scope**\n\n- Apply consistently in the SPA and human-readable API docs.\n- Do **not** rename database columns, JSON field names, path segments, or enums unless a change is local and very low risk.\n- Internal code may keep `alternative`, `criteria`, `observation`, `probe`, `vote` as implementation vocabulary.\n\n**Related product nouns (out of this glossary)**\n\n- **Application** / API keys — toolkit integrations, not decision projects.\n- **Workspace** — company/account context (signup), not a project.\n\n---\n\n## Principles\n\n1. One primary noun per concept in user-facing copy.\n2. Everyday English first; avoid MCDA jargon in the main UI.\n3. Verbs for actions, nouns for things: *compare options using factors*.\n4. Brand-safe: “choice” is product flavor (Power Choice), not the name of an option or mode.\n5. Top-level work unit: **Project**. Primary action: **Compare** / **comparison**.\n\n---\n\n## Canonical terms\n\n### 1. Container & framing\n\n| Role | Canonical | Notes |\n|------|-----------|--------|\n| Owned work unit | **Project** | Nav, lists, CRUD, routes |\n| What it’s about | **decision** | Framing only (“Structure the decision”) — not a second entity |\n| Account context | **Workspace** | Company/signup only |\n\n### 2. Project mode\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal |\n|------|-----------|----------|\n| Single winner | **Pick one** | `project_exclusive_mode = true` |\n| Full ordering | **Rank all** | `project_exclusive_mode = false` |\n| Edit labels | **Choose one winner** / **Rank all options** | OK long form on edit |\n\nAvoid in UI: Choice, Prioritization, exclusive, selection-mode.\n\n### 3. Things being compared\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n|------|-----------|-----------------|\n| Entity | **Option** | `alternative_*`, `CustomerProjectAlternatives` |\n| Count / empty states | **options** | — |\n\nAvoid in UI: alternative, choice (as the item), item.\n\n### 4. Evaluation dimensions\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n|------|-----------|-----------------|\n| Entity | **Factor** | `factor_*` |\n| Section | **Factors** | — |\n| No separate factors | **Overall only** | Synthetic factor title: **Overall** |\n| Multi | **Multiple factors** | — |\n| Templates | **Template** | Project / options / factors scopes; internal: `CustomerFactorTemplates`, `project_templates.yml` |\n\nAvoid in UI: criteria, criterion, rubric (except rare help metaphor).\n\nObservation type `criteria` means factor-vs-factor comparisons — never say “criteria” to users.\n\n### 5. Comparing (giving input)\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n|------|-----------|-----------------|\n| Verb / CTA | **Compare** / **Start comparing** | vote APIs, probe routes |\n| One unit | **comparison** | `observation`, probe |\n| Progress | **comparisons** | — |\n| Finish | **Mark complete** | — |\n| Locked | **Input closed** / **Comparisons locked** | `disabled` |\n| Status collecting | **Collecting input** | — |","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":80,"totalLines":226,"truncated":true}},"title":"docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md","time":{"start":1787313370833,"end":1787313370914}},"id":"prt_0242db687001Dudt0RIgMJJUTn","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242d9dad001Q7pHLySu24G54Z"}}
{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787313371019,"sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-168b5dd5-33f0-4bf9-b686-ccb408ae5e2c-28","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/algos.md","limit":80},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/algos.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # Ranked-group Results algorithms\n2: \n3: Canonical specification for project Results math.\n4: \n5: **Authoritative aggregate Results** (Phase 2): regularized hierarchical Bradley–Terry + Laplace (Gaussian) posterior in `src/utils/bt_inference/`. Pairings are the source of truth. Group `rank_order` is display-only / back-compat and is used only when a group has no pairings (converted to adjacent winner pairs).\n6: \n7: Implementation entry: `src/utils/vote_ranking.build_report` → `bt_inference.interface.build_report_v2`.  \n8: Sampler-shaped draws: `score_samples[S, items]` so a later MCMC engine can replace Laplace without API churn.  \n9: UI display helpers: `client/utils/ranking.ts`.\n10: \n11: User-facing terms follow `docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md` (Project · Option · Factor · Compare · Results). Keep Confidence, Agreement, Repeatability, Stability, Factor confidence, Decision leverage, and Model quality **separate** — no single blended score.\n12: \n13: Math detail: `docs_examples/Implementation Specification for Paired Adaptive question process.md` §§20–52.\n14: \n15: ---\n16: \n17: ## 0. Hierarchical BT + Laplace (authoritative)\n18: \n19: For pair `(i, j)` from participant `p` under factor `f`:\n20: \n21: ```\n22: P(i>j) = (1−λ) σ(κ (θ_pfi − θ_pfj + β_L L)) + λ/2\n23: θ_pfi = μ_fi + u_pfi\n24: ```\n25: \n26: - `μ` is the population latent (sum-to-zero per factor). `u` is a hierarchical participant deviation with strong shrinkage.\n27: - `λ`, `κ` are population-level. Unsure / skip are excluded. Ties are 0.5/0.5.\n28: - In-progress pairings are included (they are answered).\n29: - MAP + Hessian; draws from `N(θ̂, H⁻¹)`. Multi-factor overall is `U = Σ_f w_f Q_f` on those draws (`w = softmax(φ)`).\n30: - Scores in `[0, 1]` are pairwise win strength `Q_i`. Added fields: `p_best`, `p_top_n`, `rank_ci95`, `expected_rank`, `coherence`, `posterior_stability`, `diagnostics`.\n31: - Cache key = hash of the pairing set used. Compute on report/dashboard request only.\n32: \n33: The sections below document the **legacy mean-rank merge** still used for some display helpers (influence labels, multi-pass chips) and for reconstructing a provisional group order. They are **not** the aggregate ranking source.\n34: \n35: ---\n36: \n37: ## 1. Inputs\n38: \n39: Votes are stored as sort groups (`ProjectVoteGroupResult`). Aggregate inference uses **pairings** (complete + in-progress). `rank_order` is provisional.\n40: \n41: | Field | Meaning |\n42: |-------|---------|\n43: | `group_type` | `alternative` (options) or `criteria` (factors) |\n44: | `criterion_id` | Factor id for option sorts; `null` = overall options or factor-ranking group |\n45: | `pass_index` | 1-based multi-pass index |\n46: | `rank_order` | Item ids **best → worst** (position 0 = rank 0 = best) |\n47: | `comparison_count` | Pairs used inside the sort UI (audit / influence mass only) |\n48: | `pairings` | Pair outcomes from the sort UI; used to **repair** `rank_order` when present |\n49: \n50: Group kinds:\n51: \n52: 1. **Options by factor** — `alternative` + `criterion_id`\n53: 2. **Options overall** — `alternative` + null criterion (no factors / overall-only)\n54: 3. **Factors** — `criteria` (+ null criterion)\n55: \n56: ---\n57: \n58: ## 2. Rank maps and mean ranks\n59: \n60: ```\n61: effective_rank_order(group):\n62:   if stored rank_order is a full permutation AND consistent with hard pairings\n63:     (every winner ranked before its loser) → stored rank_order\n64:   else if pairings present → Copeland reconstruction (wins − losses, …)\n65:   else → stored rank_order\n66: rank_order_to_map(order): item_id → position index   # 0 = best\n67: mean_rank_maps(list of maps): item_id → average rank across maps\n68: ```\n69: \n70: Missing items in a map are omitted from that map’s average (not imputed).\n71: \n72: Preferring a consistent stored order matters: FJ pairings are a **path-dependent\n73: subset**, so Copeland on that graph alone can reorder mid/low items (e.g. reverse\n74: axis `5,4,3,2,1` → `5,4,3,1,2`) even when the client total order is correct.\n75: \n76: ### Sort UI (Ford–Johnson) — best→worst\n77: \n78: Losers of a paired winner **W** must insert at index **> index(W)** only.\n79: A prior bug inserted losers in `[0, index(W))`, which could place a known-worse\n80: item first. Fixed in `client/utils/sortCompare.ts`. Historical rows whose stored\n\n(Showing lines 1-80 of 361. Use offset=81 to continue.)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# Ranked-group Results algorithms\n\nCanonical specification for project Results math.\n\n**Authoritative aggregate Results** (Phase 2): regularized hierarchical Bradley–Terry + Laplace (Gaussian) posterior in `src/utils/bt_inference/`. Pairings are the source of truth. Group `rank_order` is display-only / back-compat and is used only when a group has no pairings (converted to adjacent winner pairs).\n\nImplementation entry: `src/utils/vote_ranking.build_report` → `bt_inference.interface.build_report_v2`.  \nSampler-shaped draws: `score_samples[S, items]` so a later MCMC engine can replace Laplace without API churn.  \nUI display helpers: `client/utils/ranking.ts`.\n\nUser-facing terms follow `docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md` (Project · Option · Factor · Compare · Results). Keep Confidence, Agreement, Repeatability, Stability, Factor confidence, Decision leverage, and Model quality **separate** — no single blended score.\n\nMath detail: `docs_examples/Implementation Specification for Paired Adaptive question process.md` §§20–52.\n\n---\n\n## 0. Hierarchical BT + Laplace (authoritative)\n\nFor pair `(i, j)` from participant `p` under factor `f`:\n","truncated":true,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/algos.md","text":"# Ranked-group Results algorithms\n\nCanonical specification for project Results math.\n\n**Authoritative aggregate Results** (Phase 2): regularized hierarchical Bradley–Terry + Laplace (Gaussian) posterior in `src/utils/bt_inference/`. Pairings are the source of truth. Group `rank_order` is display-only / back-compat and is used only when a group has no pairings (converted to adjacent winner pairs).\n\nImplementation entry: `src/utils/vote_ranking.build_report` → `bt_inference.interface.build_report_v2`.  \nSampler-shaped draws: `score_samples[S, items]` so a later MCMC engine can replace Laplace without API churn.  \nUI display helpers: `client/utils/ranking.ts`.\n\nUser-facing terms follow `docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md` (Project · Option · Factor · Compare · Results). Keep Confidence, Agreement, Repeatability, Stability, Factor confidence, Decision leverage, and Model quality **separate** — no single blended score.\n\nMath detail: `docs_examples/Implementation Specification for Paired Adaptive question process.md` §§20–52.\n\n---\n\n## 0. Hierarchical BT + Laplace (authoritative)\n\nFor pair `(i, j)` from participant `p` under factor `f`:\n\n```\nP(i>j) = (1−λ) σ(κ (θ_pfi − θ_pfj + β_L L)) + λ/2\nθ_pfi = μ_fi + u_pfi\n```\n\n- `μ` is the population latent (sum-to-zero per factor). `u` is a hierarchical participant deviation with strong shrinkage.\n- `λ`, `κ` are population-level. Unsure / skip are excluded. Ties are 0.5/0.5.\n- In-progress pairings are included (they are answered).\n- MAP + Hessian; draws from `N(θ̂, H⁻¹)`. Multi-factor overall is `U = Σ_f w_f Q_f` on those draws (`w = softmax(φ)`).\n- Scores in `[0, 1]` are pairwise win strength `Q_i`. Added fields: `p_best`, `p_top_n`, `rank_ci95`, `expected_rank`, `coherence`, `posterior_stability`, `diagnostics`.\n- Cache key = hash of the pairing set used. Compute on report/dashboard request only.\n\nThe sections below document the **legacy mean-rank merge** still used for some display helpers (influence labels, multi-pass chips) and for reconstructing a provisional group order. They are **not** the aggregate ranking source.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Inputs\n\nVotes are stored as sort groups (`ProjectVoteGroupResult`). Aggregate inference uses **pairings** (complete + in-progress). `rank_order` is provisional.\n\n| Field | Meaning |\n|-------|---------|\n| `group_type` | `alternative` (options) or `criteria` (factors) |\n| `criterion_id` | Factor id for option sorts; `null` = overall options or factor-ranking group |\n| `pass_index` | 1-based multi-pass index |\n| `rank_order` | Item ids **best → worst** (position 0 = rank 0 = best) |\n| `comparison_count` | Pairs used inside the sort UI (audit / influence mass only) |\n| `pairings` | Pair outcomes from the sort UI; used to **repair** `rank_order` when present |\n\nGroup kinds:\n\n1. **Options by factor** — `alternative` + `criterion_id`\n2. **Options overall** — `alternative` + null criterion (no factors / overall-only)\n3. **Factors** — `criteria` (+ null criterion)\n\n---\n\n## 2. Rank maps and mean ranks\n\n```\neffective_rank_order(group):\n  if stored rank_order is a full permutation AND consistent with hard pairings\n    (every winner ranked before its loser) → stored rank_order\n  else if pairings present → Copeland reconstruction (wins − losses, …)\n  else → stored rank_order\nrank_order_to_map(order): item_id → position index   # 0 = best\nmean_rank_maps(list of maps): item_id → average rank across maps\n```\n\nMissing items in a map are omitted from that map’s average (not imputed).\n\nPreferring a consistent stored order matters: FJ pairings are a **path-dependent\nsubset**, so Copeland on that graph alone can reorder mid/low items (e.g. reverse\naxis `5,4,3,2,1` → `5,4,3,1,2`) even when the client total order is correct.\n\n### Sort UI (Ford–Johnson) — best→worst\n\nLosers of a paired winner **W** must insert at index **> index(W)** only.\nA prior bug inserted losers in `[0, index(W))`, which could place a known-worse\nitem first. Fixed in `client/utils/sortCompare.ts`. Historical rows whose stored","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":80,"totalLines":361,"truncated":true}},"title":"docs_examples/algos.md","time":{"start":1787313370869,"end":1787313370953}},"id":"prt_0242db6d9001rbMDp5xvP9e4nK","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242d9dad001Q7pHLySu24G54Z"}}

title=\"Beta\")\n15:     a3 = create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Gamma\")\n16:     factor = create_factor(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Value\")\n17:     return project, a1, a2, a3, factor\n18: \n19: \n20: def _complete_issued_group(client, project_id, group: dict):\n21:     \"\"\"Build a trivial total order package for the issued group and post sorted(ids)\n26:     pairings = []\n27:     # minimal synthetic pairings covering adjacent\n28:     for i in range(len(rank) - 1):\n29:         pairings.append({\n30:             \"winner_id\": rank[i],\n31:             \"loser_id\": rank[i + 1],\n32:             \"response\": \"winner\",\n33:             \"decision_seconds\": 0.5,\n34:             \"presented_left_id\": rank[i],\n35:             \"presented_right_id\": rank[i + 1],\n36:             \"effective_rule\": \"choice\",\n37:         })\n38:     pkg = {\n39:         \"client_group_id\": group[\"client_group_id\"],\n40:         \"pass_index\": group.get(\"pass_index\") or 1,\n41:         \"group_type\": group.get(\"group_type\") or \"alternative\",\n42:         \"criterion_id\": group.get(\"criterion_id\"),\n43:         \"sort_algorithm\": group.get(\"sort_algorithm\") or \"ford_johnson\",\n44:         \"item_ids_initial\": ids,\n45:         \"rank_order\": rank,\n46:         \"pairings\": pairings,\n47:         \"algorithm_version\": \"1.0-sort\",\n48:     }\n49:     res = client.post(\n50:         \"/ws/project-votes/complete-group\",\n51:         json={\"project_id\": project_id, \"prior_group\": pkg},\n52:     )\n53:     return res\n54: \n55: \n56: def test_session_sort_group_and_report(client, account):\n57:     as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n58:     project, a1, a2, a3, factor = _seed_vote_project(account)\n59:     project_id = project.id\n60: \n61:     mine = client.get(\"/ws/project-votes/my-vote\", params={\"project_id\": project_id, \"request_next\": True})\n62:     assert mine.status_code == 200\n63:     mine_body = mine.json()\n64:     assert not mine_body.get(\"failure_reason\")\n65:     assert mine_body[\"participant_info\"][\"project_id\"] == project_id\n66:     g = mine_body.get(\"next_group\")\n67:     assert g and g.get(\"client_group_id\")\n68:     assert g.get(\"group_token\")\n69:     assert len(g[\"group_token\"]) == 10\n70:     assert g.get(\"question_budget\") or g.get(\"estimated_comparisons\")\n71:     assert g.get(\"sort_algorithm\") in (\"ford_johnson\", \"merge_sort\")\n72:     assert len(g.get(\"items\") or []) >= 2\n73: factor = create_factor(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Value\")\n    return project, a1, a2, a3, factor\n\n\ndef _complete_issued_group(client, project_id, group: dict):\n    \"\"\"Build a trivial total order package for the issued group and post it.\"\"\"\n    ids = list(group.get(\"item_ids\") or [i[\"id\"] for i in group.get(\"items\") or []])\n    assert len(ids) >= 2\n    # descending id order as rank (stable)\n    rank = sorted(ids)\n    pairings = []\n    # minimal synthetic pairings covering adjacent\n    for i in range(len(rank) - 1):\n        pairings.append({\n            \"winner_id\": rank[i],\n            \"loser_id\": rank[i + 1],\n            \"response\": \"winner\",\n            \"decision_seconds\": 0.5,\n            \"presented_left_id\": rank[i],\n            \"presented_right_id\": rank[i + 1],\n            \"effective_rule\": \"choice\",\n        })\n    pkg = {\n        \"client_group_id\": group[\"client_group_id\"],\n        \"pass_index\": group.get(\"pass_index\") or 1,\n        \"group_type\": group.get(\"group_type\") or \"alternative\",\n        \"criterion_id\": group.get(\"criterion_id\"),\n        \"sort_algorithm\": group.get(\"sort_algorithm\") or \"ford_johnson\",\n        \"item_ids_initial\": ids,\n        \"rank_order\": rank,\n        \"pairings\": pairings,\n        \"algorithm_version\": \"1.0-sort\",\n    }\n    res = client.post(\n        \"/ws/project-votes/complete-group\",\n        json={\"project_id\": project_id, \"prior_group\": pkg},\n    )\n    return res\n\n\ndef test_session_sort_group_and_report(client, account):\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n    project, a1, a2, a3, factor = _seed_vote_project(account)\n    project_id = project.id\n\n    mine = client.get(\"/ws/project-votes/my-vote\", params={\"project_id\": project_id, \"request_next\": True})\n    assert mine.status_code == 200\n    mine_body = mine.json()\n    assert not mine_body.get(\"failure_reason\")\n    assert mine_body[\"participant_info\"][\"project_id\"] == project_id\n    g = mine_body.get(\"next_group\")\n    assert g and g.get(\"client_group_id\")\n    assert g.get(\"group_token\")\n    assert len(g[\"group_token\"]) == 10\n    assert g.get(\"question_budget\") or g.get(\"estimated_comparisons\")\n    assert g.get(\"sort_algorithm\") in (\"ford_johnson\", \"merge_sort\")\n    assert len(g.get(\"items\") or []) >= 2\n    groups = mine_body.get(\"groups\") or []\n    assert any(\n        (row.get(\"group_token\") or row.get(\"client_group_id\")) == g[\"group_token\"]\n        and row.get(\"status\") == \"in_progress\"\n        for row in groups\n    )\n\n    submit = _complete_issued_group(client, project_id, g)\n    assert submit.status_code == 200\n    body = submit.json()\n    assert not body.get(\"failure_reason\"), body\n    assert body.get(\"group_info\")\n    assert body[\"group_info\"][\"client_group_id\"] == g[\"client_group_id\"]\n\n    # idempotent replay\n    replay = _complete_issued_group(client, project_id, g)\n    assert replay.status_code == 200\n    assert replay.json()[\"group_info\"][\"id\"] == body[\"group_info\"][\"id\"]\n\n    report = client.post(\n        \"/ws/project-votes/project-report\",\n        json={\"project_id\": project_id, \"include_participants\": True},\n    )\n    assert report.status_code == 200\n    rb = report.json()\n    assert not rb.get(\"failure_reason\"), rb\n    assert rb[\"report\"][\"unique_participants\"] \"/ws/project-votes/report-pivot-detail\",\n        json={\"project_id\": project_id, \"primary_axis\": \"alternatives\", \"row_id\": a1.id},\n    )\n    assert detail.status_code == 200\n    assert len(parts.json().get(\"participant_info_list\") or []) >= 1\n\n\ndef test_save_group_partial_and_resume(client, account):\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n    project, a1, a2, a3, _factor = list(g.get(\"item_ids\") or [i[\"id\"] for i in g.get(\"items\") or []])\n    pairing = {\n        \"winner_id\": ids[0],\n        \"loser_id\": ids[1],\n        \"response\": \"winner\",\n option_batch_count(option_n: int, question_budget: int) -> int:\n    if option_n < 2:\n        return 0\n    budget = max(1, int(question_budget or 1))\n    if ford_johnson_budget(option_n) <= _group_dicts(groups: list | None) -> list[dict]:\n    return [_as_dict(g) for g in (groups or [])]\n\n\ndef max_completed_pass(groups: list | None) -> int:\n    done = _complete_group_dicts(groups)\n    if not done:\n        return 0\n    return max(int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) for g in done)\n\n\ndef completed_keys_for_pass(groups: list | None, pass_index: int) -> set[str]:\n    keys: set[str] \"value\"):\n            gt = gt.value\n        cid = g.get(\"criterion_id\")\n        keys.add(_group_key(str(gt), cid, pass_index, int(g.get(\"batch_index\") or 0)))\n    return keys\n\n\ndef _group_item_ids(g: dict) -> set[int]:\n    raw = g.get(\"item_ids_initial\") or g.get(\"item_ids\") or g.get(\"rank_order\") or []\n    return {int(x) for x in raw}\n\n\ndef channel_covered_item_ids(\n    groups: list | None,\n    *,\n    pass_index: int,\n    group_type: str,\n    criterion_id: int | None,\n) -> set[int]:\n    covered: set[int] = set()\n    for g in _complete_group_dicts(groups):\n        if int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) != pass_index:\n            continue\n        gt = g.get(\"group_type\")\n        if hasattr(gt, \"value\"):\n            gt = gt.value\n        if str(gt) != str(group_type):\n            continue\n        if g.get(\"criterion_id\") != criterion_id:\n            continue\n        covered |= _group_item_ids(g)\n    return covered\n\n\ndef required_slots_for_pass(\n    *,\n    pass_index: int,\n    alternative_ids: list[int],\n    factor_ids: list[int],\n    option_question_budget: int | None = None,\n    factor_question_budget: int | None = None,\n    rng: random.Random | None = None,\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Option groups first (shuffled by caller), then factor group if needed.\"\"\"\n    rng = rng or random.Random()\n    slots: list[dict] = []\n    opt_budget = int(option_question_budget or clamp_questions_per_group_stored(None))\n    fac_budget = int(factor_question_budget or clamp_questions_per_group_stored(None))\n    channels: list[tuple[str, int | None, list[int], int]] = []\n    if factor_ids:\n        for fid in factor_ids:\n            channels.append((\"alternative\", int(fid), list(alternative_ids), opt_budget))\n    else:\n        channels.append((\"alternative\", None, list(alternative_ids), opt_budget))\n    if len(factor_ids) >= 2:\n        channels.append((\"criteria\", None, list(factor_ids), fac_budget))\n    for group_type, criterion_id, item_ids, budget in channels:\n        n = len(item_ids)\n        q = clamp_questions_per_group(budget, n) if n >= 2 else 0\n        batches = partition_item_ids(item_ids, q or 1, rng) if n >= 2 else []\n        for batch_i, batch_ids in enumerate(batches):\n            bq = clamp_questions_per_group(budget, len(batch_ids))\n            slots.append({\n                \"group_type\": group_type,\n                \"criterion_id\": criterion_id,\n                \"pass_index\": pass_index,\n                \"batch_index\": batch_i,\n                \"item_ids\": list(batch_ids),\n                \"question_budget\": bq,\n            })\n    return   participant = _resolve_share_participant(session, request, link_info, share_result, access_info)\n    personal = _personal_vote_bundle(session, participant)\n    next_group = None\n    progress: requested_target_share_operation=ShareAccessOperation.vote,\n    )\n    share_result, link_info, access_info = await share_link_validate_and_log(\n        session=session,  project = CustomerProject.get_customer_project_by_id_system(\n        session=session, customer_id=link_info.customer_id, id=link_info.shared_entity_db_id, clear_lock=False\n    )\n    if project is  return ExternalGroupResult(failure_reason=blocked, share_result_status=share_result)\n    participant = _resolve_share_participant(session, request, link_info, share_result, access_info)\n    if _load_project_summary(session, link_info)\n    issued = _issue_next(session, project=project, participant=participant, alts=alts, factors=factors)\n    return ExternalGroupResult(\n progress=issued.get(\"progress\") or {},\n        session_complete=bool(issued.get(\"session_complete\")),\n        project_settings=_project_settings(project),\n )\n","lineStart":420,"lineEnd":519,"totalLines":945,"truncated":true}},"title":"src/api/app_shared_link_ext_access.py","time":{"start":1787313392598,"end":1787313392928}},"id":"prt_0242e0ab0001V8B3NlNRD93LSe","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242de1c80016UGdUqW2q7Y9dI"}}

state.answers.length - 1\n  if (remainingAfter <= 0) return true\n  const next: SchedulerState = {\n    ...state,\n    answers: [...state.answers, {\n      itemA: pair[0],\n      itemB: pair[1],\n      winnerId: pair[0],\n      response: 'winner',\n      presentedLeftId: pair[0],\n      presentedRightId: pair[1],\n      decisionSeconds: 0,\n    }],\n    lastPair: pair,\n  }\n  const legal = enumerateLegalPairs(next)\n  if (!legal.length) return false\n  if (state.passIndex === 1) {\n    const unseen = unseenItems(next)\n    if (unseen.size > remainingAfter * 2) return false\n    if (unseen.size === 1) {\n      const u = [...unseen][0]\n      const canCover = legal.some(([a, b]) => a === u || b === u)\n      if (!canCover && remainingAfter === 1) return false\n    }\n  }\n  return true\n}\n\nfunction acquisitionScore(state: SchedulerState, pair: [number, number], scores: Map<number, number>, ranks: Map<number, number>): number {\n  const [a, b] = pair\n  const sa = scores.get(a) || 0\n  const sb = scores.get(b) || 0\n  const p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-(sa - sb)))\n  const I = 4 * p * (1 - p)\n  const ra = ranks.get(a) || 1\n  const rb = ranks.get(b) || 1\n  const n = state.items.length\n  const topN = Math.max(1, Math.min(state.target.n || 1, Math.floor(n / 2) || 1))\n  let T = 1\n  if (state.target.mode === 'winner') {\n    const Ta = Math.exp(-(ra - 1) / 2)\n    const Tb = Math.exp(-(rb - 1) / 2)\n    T = (Ta + Tb) / 2\n    if (ra === 1 || rb === 1) T += 0.35\n  } else if (state.target.mode === 'top_n') {\n    const ordered = [...state.items].sort((x, y) => (ranks.get(x) || 0) - (ranks.get(y) || 0))\n    const boundary = ordered.length > topN\n      ? ((scores.get(ordered[topN - 1]) || 0) + (scores.get(ordered[topN]) || 0)) / 2\n      : (scores.get(ordered[ordered.length - 1]) || 0)\n    const Da = Math.exp(-Math.abs(sa - boundary) / 0.75)\n    const Db = Math.exp(-Math.abs(sb - boundary) / 0.75)\n    T = (Da + Db) / 2\n    const straddles = (ra <= topN) !== (rb <= topN)\n    if (straddles) T += 0.55\n  } else {\n    T = Math.exp(-(Math.abs(ra - rb) - 1) / 2.5)\n  }\n  const hist = historicalStats(state.priorAnswers, a, b)\n  const R = 1 / Math.sqrt(1 + 0.65 * hist.count)\n  const exposure = new Map<number, number>()\n  for (const id of state.items) exposure.set(id, 0)\n  for (const ans of state.answers) {\n    exposure.set(ans.itemA, (exposure.get(ans.itemA) || 0) + 1)\n    exposure.set(ans.itemB, (exposure.get(ans.itemB) || 0) + 1)\n  }\n  const E = 1 + 0.15 * (2 - Math.min(exposure.get(a) || 0, 2) - Math.min(exposure.get(b) || 0, 2))\n  const edges: Array<[number, number]> = []\n  for (const ans of [...state.priorAnswers, ...state.answers]) {\n    if (ans.response === 'unsure' || ans.response === 'skipped') continue\n    edges.push([ans.itemA, ans.itemB])\n  }\n  const comps = components(state.items, edges)\n  const inSame = comps.some(c => c.includes(a) && c.includes(b))\n  const G = inSame ? 1 : 2.4\n  let bonus = 0\n  if (hist.count >= 2 && hist.winsA && hist.winsB) bonus += 0.25\n  return I * T * R * E * G + bonus\n}\n\nexport function selectNextPair(state: SchedulerState): [number, number] | null {\n  if (state.answers.length >= state.questionBudget) return null\n  if (state.items.length < 2) return null\n  const pairCap = maxUniquePairs(state.items.length)\n  if (state.answers.length >= pairCap) return null\n  let candidates = enumerateLegalPairs(state)","lineStart":150,"lineEnd":249,"totalLines":309,"truncated":true}},"title":"client/utils/pairScheduler.ts","time":{"start":1787313402097,"end":1787313402566}},"id":"prt_0242e2f80001YyvXZIOb7frc1i","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242e1b81001saIvI5fwKUWqdC"}}
{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787313402685,"sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-3d009ecf-0555-41f4-a996-15ee890bdb19-50","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/algos.md","offset":300,"limit":70},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/algos.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n300: \n301: Stored as:\n302: \n303: - `coherence_raw` ∈ \\[−1, 1\\]  \n304: - `coherence` = `(raw + 1) / 2` ∈ \\[0, 1\\] for UI “align %”\n305: \n306: ---\n307: \n308: ## 12. Multi-pass averaging (important)\n309: \n310: Within a channel, multiple `pass_index` rows are **mean-averaged** before scoring:\n311: \n312: ```\n313: mean_rank_i = average of rank_i across passes\n314: ```\n315: \n316: A leader can be a **compromise** of disagreeing passes (e.g. #3 then #2 beats #1 then #7).  \n317: `pass_consistency` on by-factor rows is the multi-pass JS-agreement for that channel — low means the mean rank is unstable across repeats.\n318: \n319: ---\n320: \n321: ## 13. Report payload (contract)\n322: \n323: `build_report` returns (key fields):\n324: \n325: | Key | Content |\n326: |-----|---------|\n327: | `option_ranking` | Adjusted (factor-weighted) options; `score` ∈ \\[0,1\\] |\n328: | `option_ranking_equal` | Equal factor weights |\n329: | `factor_ranking` | Factors with `score`, `normalizedWeight`, conf/stab % |\n330: | `factor_weights` | id → weight |\n331: | `by_factor` | fid → option ranking under that factor |\n332: | `metrics` | stability, stability_basis, confidence, multi_pass, factor_divergence, agreement, depth, completion, counts (fractions) |\n333: | `dispersion` | see §9 |\n334: | `participants[]` | `option_ranking`, `ranking` alias, `leader`, stability/confidence (0–1), coherence, counts |\n335: | `pivot` | by_participant / by_factor / by_alternative rollups |\n336: | `results.importance_adjusted` / `equal_weight` / `leader` / `criterion_weights` | Share-report compatibility |\n337: | `total_observations` / `comparison_count` | Sort-internal pair counts (not pair-model evidence) |\n338: \n339: Metrics at the API boundary are **0–1 fractions**. Clients convert with `asMetricPercent` / Results `pct()`.\n340: \n341: ---\n342: \n343: ## 14. What not to do\n344: \n345: - Do **not** aggregate Results from `pairings` or legacy pair observations.  \n346: - Do **not** emit rank-distance as `score` (e.g. 4.0 for five options).  \n347: - Do **not** feed 0–1 metrics into labels that expect 0–100 without conversion.  \n348: - Do **not** double-apply β when computing influence (`mass = w × n`, not `w × n^β`).  \n349: - Do **not** use cross-factor option-list agreement as stability or multi-pass.  \n350: - Do **not** treat high factor divergence as a quality failure.\n351: \n352: ---\n353: \n354: ## 15. Change control\n355: \n356: When changing formulas:\n357: \n358: 1. Update this file and `src/utils/vote_ranking.py` together.  \n359: 2. Extend `src/tests/test_vote_ranking_probes.py` (scores ∈ \\[0,1\\], stability/confidence ranges, influence).  \n360: 3. Keep UI helpers in `client/utils/ranking.ts` aligned (`formatPercent`, `asMetricPercent`, `mapServerRanking`).  \n361: 4. Bump `settings.VERSION` on shippable changes.\n\n(End of file - total 361 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"\nStored as:\n\n- `coherence_raw` ∈ \\[−1, 1\\]  \n- `coherence` = `(raw + 1) / 2` ∈ \\[0, 1\\] for UI “align %”\n\n---\n\n## 12. Multi-pass averaging (important)\n\nWithin a channel, multiple `pass_index` rows are **mean-averaged** before scoring:\n\n```\nmean_rank_i = average of rank_i across passes\n```\n\nA leader can be a **compromise** of disagreeing passes (e.g. #3 then #2 beats #1 then #7).  \n`pass_consistency` on by-factor rows is the multi-pass JS-agreement for that channel — low means the mean rank is unstable across repeats.\n\n---","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/algos.md","text":"\nStored as:\n\n- `coherence_raw` ∈ \\[−1, 1\\]  \n- `coherence` = `(raw + 1) / 2` ∈ \\[0, 1\\] for UI “align %”\n\n---\n\n## 12. Multi-pass averaging (important)\n\nWithin a channel, multiple `pass_index` rows are **mean-averaged** before scoring:\n\n```\nmean_rank_i = average of rank_i across passes\n```\n\nA leader can be a **compromise** of disagreeing passes (e.g. #3 then #2 beats #1 then #7).  \n`pass_consistency` on by-factor rows is the multi-pass JS-agreement for that channel — low means the mean rank is unstable across repeats.\n\n---\n\n## 13. Report payload (contract)\n\n`build_report` returns (key fields):\n\n| Key | Content |\n|-----|---------|\n| `option_ranking` | Adjusted (factor-weighted) options; `score` ∈ \\[0,1\\] |\n| `option_ranking_equal` | Equal factor weights |\n| `factor_ranking` | Factors with `score`, `normalizedWeight`, conf/stab % |\n| `factor_weights` | id → weight |\n| `by_factor` | fid → option ranking under that factor |\n| `metrics` | stability, stability_basis, confidence, multi_pass, factor_divergence, agreement, depth, completion, counts (fractions) |\n| `dispersion` | see §9 |\n| `participants[]` | `option_ranking`, `ranking` alias, `leader`, stability/confidence (0–1), coherence, counts |\n| `pivot` | by_participant / by_factor / by_alternative rollups |\n| `results.importance_adjusted` / `equal_weight` / `leader` / `criterion_weights` | Share-report compatibility |\n| `total_observations` / `comparison_count` | Sort-internal pair counts (not pair-model evidence) |\n\nMetrics at the API boundary are **0–1 fractions**. Clients convert with `asMetricPercent` / Results `pct()`.\n\n---\n\n## 14. What not to do\n\n- Do **not** aggregate Results from `pairings` or legacy pair observations.  \n- Do **not** emit rank-distance as `score` (e.g. 4.0 for five options).  \n- Do **not** feed 0–1 metrics into labels that expect 0–100 without conversion.  \n- Do **not** double-apply β when computing influence (`mass = w × n`, not `w × n^β`).  \n- Do **not** use cross-factor option-list agreement as stability or multi-pass.  \n- Do **not** treat high factor divergence as a quality failure.\n\n---\n\n## 15. Change control\n\nWhen changing formulas:\n\n1. Update this file and `src/utils/vote_ranking.py` together.  \n2. Extend `src/tests/test_vote_ranking_probes.py` (scores ∈ \\[0,1\\], stability/confidence ranges, influence).  \n3. Keep UI helpers in `client/utils/ranking.ts` aligned (`formatPercent`, `asMetricPercent`, `mapServerRanking`).  \n4. Bump `settings.VERSION` on shippable changes.","lineStart":300,"lineEnd":361,"totalLines":361,"truncated":false}},"title":"docs_examples/algos.md","time":{"start":1787313402479,"end":1787313402674}},"id":"prt_0242e30f9001d7Vh6TSy8JiPH7","sessionID":"ses_fdbd2e5c0ffeIGb90O3O1UoOdD","messageID":"msg_0242e1b81001saIvI5fwKUWqdC"}}

Do not change pair scheduling, Results metrics, or stop-at-threshold behavior.\\n\\nToday: `probe.vue` and `ShareVoteSession.vue` already show **This group** and **Overall** (count vs min passes). Share complete-view uses `metricLabel()` on server `submitter_summary.confidence` (multi-pass only). Project Confidence in `vote_ranking.py` is multi-participant — **not** this bar. Client only holds the current group plus same-channel `prior_pairings`.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 1. Implementation steps\\n\\n1. **Add `client/utils/rankingConfidence.ts`** (pure, tested)\\n   - Input: every channel’s pairings (option-under-factor + factor-vs-factor if ≥2 factors), item ids, ranking target.\\n   - Output: `{ value: 0–1, level, label, color }`.\\n   - **Score (recommended):** equal-weight mean of per-channel scores:\\n     - **Coverage ~70%** — decisive pairs (`winner`/`tie`) ÷ pairs needed for that channel’s target (`question_budget` / connected ranking). `unsure`/`skip` do not add coverage.\\n     - **Consistency ~20%** — repeated-pair agreement, else same-channel multi-pass JS agreement when ≥2 rank orders exist; **omit** this term on first pass (don’t force None).\\n     - **Decisiveness ~10%** — top-vs-next gap from existing `fitProvisionalScores()` in `pairScheduler.ts`.\\n   - Missing factor ranking (multi-factor) keeps the mean from reaching Strong.\\n   - Map to six levels: **None / Low / Moderate / Fair / Good / Strong** (None at 0; then ~20% bands). Do **not** reuse or change `metricLabel()` (Sparse/Early/Building/Solid/Strong on Results).\\n   - Color: interpolate `--pc-danger` → `--pc-warning` → `--pc-succe...\n\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/add-a-your-result-confidence-level-5jjvv2/2026-08-21T10-28-17-777Z-plan-3fu8nz.response.md:\n  Line 36: 5. **Tests:** `client/utils/rankingConfidence.test.ts` (node:test, same pattern as `ranking.test.ts`) — empty→None; options-only vs options+factors; unsure doesn’t inflate; undo drops score; six-level boundaries. { test } from 'node:test'\\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict'\\nimport {\\n  factorDetailsCopy,\\n  hasCustomComparisonQuestion,\\n  optionCompareQuestion,\\n  optionCompareTitle,\\n
